According to guideline AFTD2022-BLK-COM-005, a person with such blackouts is not fit to hold an unconditional licence if the mechanism cannot be diagnosed as syncope, seizure, or another condition. Furthermore, for a conditional licence to be considered under these specific circumstances (two+ events separated by 24h), the patient must have had no further blackouts for at least 10 years. The patient's record shows 9.99 years since the last event, which fails this prerequisite. Additionally, the mechanism remains undetermined after investigation.
